    [System.Serializable]
    public class CronExpression : System.Runtime.Serialization.IDeserializationCallback, System.Runtime.Serialization.ISerializable
    {
        protected const int AllSpec = 99;
        protected const int AllSpecInt = 99;
        protected const int DayOfMonth = 3;
        protected const int DayOfWeek = 5;
        protected const int Hour = 2;
        protected const int Minute = 1;
        protected const int Month = 4;
        protected const int NoSpec = 98;
        protected const int NoSpecInt = 98;
        protected const int Second = 0;
        protected const int Year = 6;
        protected bool calendardayOfMonth;
        protected bool calendardayOfWeek;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> daysOfMonth;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> daysOfWeek;
        protected int everyNthWeek;
        protected bool expressionParsed;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> hours;
        protected bool lastdayOfWeek;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> minutes;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> months;
        protected int nthdayOfWeek;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> seconds;
        protected System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> years;
        public static readonly int MaxYear;
        public CronExpression(string cronExpression) { }
        protected CronExpression(System.Runtime.Serialization.SerializationInfo info, System.Runtime.Serialization.StreamingContext context) { }
        public CronExpression(string cronExpression, int hashSeed) { }
        public CronExpression(string cronExpression, string hashKey) { }
        public string CronExpressionString { get; }
        public System.TimeZoneInfo TimeZone { get; set; }
        protected virtual void AddToSet(int val, int end, int incr, int type) { }
        protected void BuildExpression(string expression) { }
        protected virtual int CheckNext(int pos, string s, int val, int type) { }
        public object Clone() { }
        public bool Equals(Quartz.CronExpression other) { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        protected virtual string GetExpressionSetSummary(System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<int> data) { }
        public virtual string GetExpressionSummary() { }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetFinalFireTime() { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        protected virtual int GetLastDayOfMonth(int monthNum, int year) { }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetNextInvalidTimeAfter(System.DateTimeOffset date) { }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetNextValidTimeAfter(System.DateTimeOffset date) { }
        [System.Security.SecurityCritical]
        public void GetObjectData(System.Runtime.Serialization.SerializationInfo info, System.Runtime.Serialization.StreamingContext context) { }
        protected virtual System.Collections.Generic.SortedSet<int> GetSet(int type) { }
        protected virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetTime(int sc, int mn, int hr, int dayofmn, int mon) { }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetTimeAfter(System.DateTimeOffset afterTimeUtc) { }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? GetTimeBefore(System.DateTimeOffset endTime) { }
        protected virtual bool IsLeapYear(int year) { }
        public virtual bool IsSatisfiedBy(System.DateTimeOffset dateUtc) { }
        public void OnDeserialization(object? sender) { }
        protected virtual int SkipWhiteSpace(int i, string s) { }
        protected virtual int StoreExpressionVals(int pos, string s, int type) { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        protected static System.DateTimeOffset CreateDateTimeWithoutMillis(System.DateTimeOffset time) { }
        public static bool IsValidExpression(string cronExpression) { }
        public static bool IsValidExpression(string cronExpression, string hashKey) { }
        public static string ResolveHash(string cronExpression, int hashSeed) { }
        public static string ResolveHash(string cronExpression, string hashKey) { }
        protected static System.DateTimeOffset SetCalendarHour(System.DateTimeOffset date, int hour) { }
        public static void ValidateExpression(string cronExpression) { }
        public static void ValidateExpression(string cronExpression, string hashKey) { }
    }

        public Quartz.ExecutionLimits Unlimited(string group) { }
    }

    public interface IJob
    {
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task Execute(Quartz.IJobExecutionContext context);
    }
    public interface IJobConfigurator
    {
        Quartz.JobBuilder DisallowConcurrentExecution(bool concurrentExecutionDisallowed = true);
        Quartz.JobBuilder PersistJobDataAfterExecution(bool persistJobDataAfterExecution = true);
        Quartz.JobBuilder RequestRecovery(bool shouldRecover = true);
        Quartz.JobBuilder SetJobData(Quartz.JobDataMap? newJobDataMap);
        Quartz.JobBuilder StoreDurably(bool durability = true);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(Quartz.JobDataMap newJobDataMap);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, System.Guid value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, bool value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, char value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, double value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, float value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, int value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, long value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder UsingJobData(string key, string value);
        Quartz.JobBuilder WithDescription(string? description);
        Quartz.JobBuilder WithIdentity(Quartz.JobKey key);
        Quartz.JobBuilder WithIdentity(string name);
        Quartz.JobBuilder WithIdentity(string name, string group);
    }
    public interface IJobDetail
    {
        bool ConcurrentExecutionDisallowed { get; }
        string? Description { get; }
        bool Durable { get; }
        Quartz.JobDataMap JobDataMap { get; }
        System.Type JobType { get; }
        Quartz.JobKey Key { get; }
        bool PersistJobDataAfterExecution { get; }
        bool RequestsRecovery { get; }
        Quartz.IJobDetail Clone();
        Quartz.JobBuilder GetJobBuilder();
    }
    public interface IJobExecutionContext
    {
        Quartz.ICalendar? Calendar { get; }
        System.Threading.CancellationToken CancellationToken { get; }
        string FireInstanceId { get; }
        System.DateTimeOffset FireTimeUtc { get; }
        Quartz.IJobDetail JobDetail { get; }
        Quartz.IJob JobInstance { get; }
        System.TimeSpan JobRunTime { get; }
        Quartz.JobDataMap MergedJobDataMap { get; }
        System.DateTimeOffset? NextFireTimeUtc { get; }
        System.DateTimeOffset? PreviousFireTimeUtc { get; }
        bool Recovering { get; }
        Quartz.TriggerKey RecoveringTriggerKey { get; }
        int RefireCount { get; }
        object? Result { get; set; }
        System.DateTimeOffset? ScheduledFireTimeUtc { get; }
        Quartz.IScheduler Scheduler { get; }
        Quartz.ITrigger Trigger { get; }
        object? Get(object key);
        void Put(object key, object objectValue);
    }
    public interface IJobListener
    {
        string Name { get; }
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task JobExecutionVetoed(Quartz.IJobExecutionContext context, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);

        System.Threading.Tasks.Task JobWasExecuted(Quartz.IJobExecutionContext context, Quartz.JobExecutionException? jobException, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
    }

    }
    public interface IListenerManager
    {

        bool AddTriggerListenerMatcher(string listenerName, Quartz.IMatcher<Quartz.TriggerKey> matcher);

        System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyCollection<Quartz.IJobListener> GetJobListeners();

        Quartz.ITriggerListener GetTriggerListener(string name);

        System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyCollection<Quartz.ITriggerListener> GetTriggerListeners();
        bool RemoveJobListener(string name);
        bool RemoveJobListenerMatcher(string listenerName, Quartz.IMatcher<Quartz.JobKey> matcher);
        bool RemoveSchedulerListener(Quartz.ISchedulerListener schedulerListener);
        bool RemoveTriggerListener(string name);
        bool RemoveTriggerListenerMatcher(string listenerName, Quartz.IMatcher<Quartz.TriggerKey> matcher);
        bool SetJobListenerMatchers(string listenerName, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyCollection<Quartz.IMatcher<Quartz.JobKey>> matchers);
        bool SetTriggerListenerMatchers(string listenerName, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyCollection<Quartz.IMatcher<Quartz.TriggerKey>> matchers);
    }
    public interface IMatcher<T>
        where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T>
    {
        bool Equals(object obj);
        int GetHashCode();
        bool IsMatch(T key);
    }

        {

    public abstract class SimplePropertiesTriggerPersistenceDelegateSupport : Quartz.Impl.AdoJobStore.ITriggerPersistenceDelegate
    {
        protected const string ColumnBoolProp1 = "BOOL_PROP_1";

namespace Quartz.Impl.Matchers
{
    [System.Serializable]
    public class AndMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected AndMatcher(Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> leftOperand, Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> rightOperand) { }
        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> LeftOperand { get; }
        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> RightOperand { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.AndMatcher<T> And<T>(Quartz.IMatcher<T> leftOperand, Quartz.IMatcher<T> rightOperand)
            where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T> { }
    }
    public class EverythingMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected EverythingMatcher() { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.EverythingMatcher<Quartz.JobKey> AllJobs() { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.EverythingMatcher<Quartz.TriggerKey> AllTriggers() { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class GroupMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected GroupMatcher(string compareTo, Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator compareWith) { }
        protected override string GetValue(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.GroupMatcher<TKey> AnyGroup() { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.GroupMatcher<TKey> GroupContains(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.GroupMatcher<TKey> GroupEndsWith(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.GroupMatcher<TKey> GroupEquals(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.GroupMatcher<TKey> GroupStartsWith(string compareTo) { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class KeyMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected KeyMatcher(TKey compareTo) { }
        public TKey CompareToValue { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.KeyMatcher<T> KeyEquals<T>(T compareTo)
            where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T> { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class NameMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected NameMatcher(string compareTo, Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator compareWith) { }
        protected override string GetValue(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.NameMatcher<TKey> NameContains(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.NameMatcher<TKey> NameEndsWith(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.NameMatcher<TKey> NameEquals(string compareTo) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.NameMatcher<TKey> NameStartsWith(string compareTo) { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class NotMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected NotMatcher(Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> operand) { }
        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> Operand { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.NotMatcher<T> Not<T>(Quartz.IMatcher<T> operand)
            where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T> { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class OrMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected OrMatcher(Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> leftOperand, Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> rightOperand) { }
        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> LeftOperand { get; }
        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> RightOperand { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.OrMatcher<T> Or<T>(Quartz.IMatcher<T> leftOperand, Quartz.IMatcher<T> rightOperand)
            where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T> { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public abstract class StringMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected StringMatcher() { }
        protected StringMatcher(string compareTo, Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator compareWith) { }
        public string CompareToValue { get; }
        public Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator CompareWithOperator { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        protected abstract string GetValue(TKey key);
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public abstract class StringOperator : System.IEquatable<Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator>
    {
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Anything;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Contains;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator EndsWith;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Equality;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator StartsWith;
        protected StringOperator() { }
        public bool Equals(Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator? other) { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public abstract bool Evaluate(string value, string compareTo);
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
    }
}

    }

    {

    [System.Serializable]
    public class DirtyFlagMap<TKey, TValue> : System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>, System.Collections.Generic.IDictionary<TKey, TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyCollection<System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyDictionary<TKey, TValue>, System.Collections.ICollection, System.Collections.IDictionary, System.Collections.IEnumerable, System.Runtime.Serialization.ISerializable
        where TKey :  notnull
    {
        public DirtyFlagMap() { }
        public DirtyFlagMap(int initialCapacity) { }
        protected DirtyFlagMap(System.Runtime.Serialization.SerializationInfo info, System.Runtime.Serialization.StreamingContext context) { }
        public virtual int Count { get; }
        public virtual bool Dirty { get; }
        public virtual bool IsEmpty { get; }
        public virtual bool IsFixedSize { get; }
        public virtual bool IsReadOnly { get; }
        public virtual bool IsSynchronized { get; }
        public virtual TValue this[TKey key] { get; set; }
        public virtual System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<TKey> Keys { get; }
        public virtual object SyncRoot { get; }
        public virtual System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<TValue> Values { get; }
        public virtual System.Collections.Generic.IDictionary<TKey, TValue> WrappedMap { get; }
        public void Add(System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue> item) { }
        public virtual void Add(TKey key, [System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.AllowNull] TValue value) { }
        public void Add(object key, object? value) { }
        public virtual void Clear() { }
